# Implementation source log

This log continues the spec's source log (Appendix A of `docs/spec/openftgs-spec.md`) for the implementation phase. Every source consulted gets one row: what it was and what was taken from it. Reading this repository and the spec needs no row.

If a forbidden source is met by accident, its row starts with **Incident** and says what was seen, that reading stopped, and that nothing was taken.

| # | Date | Source | What was taken |

| 6 | 2026-10-11 | gsplat 1.5.3 as installed from PyPI (Apache-2.0; `gsplat-1.5.3.dist-info/licenses/LICENSE` read first): `gsplat/cuda/_torch_impl.py`, `_eval_sh_bases_fast` and `_spherical_harmonics`; `gsplat/cuda/_wrapper.py`, the `spherical_harmonics` signature and docstring | The SH basis is ordered by band l and, within a band, m = −l … l; signs keep the Condon–Shortley phase (l = 1 is C₁·(−y, z, −x); the odd-m terms of l = 2 and l = 3 are negative); directions are normalized inside; `spherical_harmonics(degrees_to_use, dirs [..., 3], coeffs [..., K, 3])` returns the plain sum over bases, without the +0.5 offset or a clamp. No code copied: `openftgs.sh` is written from the Cartesian table of the real SH, not from gsplat's recurrence (from #15: M2 · Motion, temporal opacity and spherical harmonics) |

Neither project's documentation nor source was opened (from #15: M2 · Motion, temporal opacity and spherical harmonics) |

the exported stage functions and their signatures; the culls in order (depth, `det <= 0`, opacity below `ALPHA_THRESHOLD` = `1.f/255.f`), the extent `min(3.33, sqrt(2·__logf(opacity/ALPHA_THRESHOLD)))`, `ceilf` radii, the cull when both radii are ≤ `radius_clip`, the off-image test; unpacked `means2d` is allocated with `at::empty` and written only for rows that pass every cull; packed rows are written camera-major in ascending row order by a block scan, with int64 ids; `rasterize_to_pixels` checks the background against `means2d.shape[:-2]`, which rejects a [C, 3] background in packed mode (Q-38); the forward kernel's 0.999 clamp with `__expf`, skip and exclusive stop; the Jacobian-clamp and α_max gradients pass inclusively at the bound; the projection's backward gives no opacity gradient; the SH backward returns the direction gradient; the CUDA code is JIT-built with fast math unless `NO_FAST_MATH=1`, which is why the backends differ in the last bits (Q-39) (from #44: M5 · gsplat renderer) |

Parallel rays through the principal point give a null vector with h₄ = 0 exactly; through other pixels, h₄ of about 10⁻¹⁶ and a finite point about 10¹⁵ away; a zero matrix gives (0, 0, 0, 1).
